### EAR OS — ARCHITECTURAL SINGULARITY (SCHEMA & SIMULATION)

#### 1. Canonical Schema (`prisma/schema.prisma`)
```prisma
generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

// --- CORE IDENTITY & ACCESS ---

enum Role {
  EXPLORADOR
  ARQUITECTO
  OPERADOR
  ADMIN
  ARTIST
  COMMANDER
  FLEET_OPERATOR
  PROVIDER
  AFFILIATE
  CLIENT
}

enum ActorType {
  ARTIST
  PROVIDER
  AFFILIATE
  OPERATOR
  CLIENT
  ADMIN
}

model User {
  id                    String               @id
  email                 String               @unique
  role                  Role                 @default(EXPLORADOR)
  rank                  String               @default("NIVEL_0_EXPLORADOR")
  isPacienteCero        Boolean              @default(false) @map("is_paciente_cero")
  
  // Perfiles Especializados (1:1)
  artistProfile         ArtistProfile?
  providerProfile       ProviderProfile?
  affiliateProfile      AffiliateProfile?
  clientProfile         ClientProfile?
  
  // Multi-Tenancy
  memberships           WorkspaceMember[]
  
  // Actividad & Logística
  waybillParticipations WaybillParticipant[]
  storageAssets         StorageAsset[]
  contracts             SmartContract[]
  wallet                AuraWallet?
  ledgerEntries         CommissionLedger[]
  
  createdAt             DateTime             @default(now())
  updatedAt             DateTime             @updatedAt

  @@map("ear_users")
}

// --- MULTI-TENANCY SOBERANA ---

model Workspace {
  id            String            @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  name          String
  slug          String            @unique
  members       WorkspaceMember[]
  units         FleetUnit[]
  assets        StorageAsset[]
  waybills      Waybill[]
  
  createdAt     DateTime          @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime          @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("ear_workspaces")
}

model WorkspaceMember {
  id            String            @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  workspaceId   String            @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")
  workspace     Workspace         @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id])
  userId        String            @map("user_id")
  user          User              @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  
  actorType     ActorType         @default(OPERATOR) @map("actor_type")
  role          String            @default("MEMBER")
  status        String            @default("ACTIVE")
  
  createdAt     DateTime          @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime          @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@unique([workspaceId, userId, actorType])
  @@map("ear_workspace_members")
}

// --- VERTICAL PERFILES (MARKETPLACE) ---

model ArtistProfile {
  id            String          @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId        String          @unique
  user          User            @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  
  slug          String          @unique
  displayName   String
  bio           String?
  genres        String[]
  mediaKitUrl   String?
  status        String          @default("DRAFT")
  
  riders        TechnicalRider[]
  calendar      CalendarBlock[]
  
  createdAt     DateTime        @default(now())
  updatedAt     DateTime        @updatedAt

  @@map("ear_artist_profiles")
}

model ProviderProfile {
  id                  String           @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId              String?          @unique
  user                User?            @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  
  name                String           @unique
  category            String?
  location            String?
  roi_guarantee_score Float            @default(0.0)
  roiProjected        Float            @default(0.0)
  auraLevel           Float            @default(0.0)
  technicalReliability Float           @default(0.0)
  
  createdAt           DateTime         @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt           DateTime         @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("ear_provider_profiles")
}

model AffiliateProfile {
  id            String          @id @default(cuid())
  userId        String          @unique
  user          User            @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  
  affiliateHash String          @unique
  tier          Int             @default(1)
  leadsReferred Int             @default(0)
  conversionRate Float          @default(0.0)
  
  createdAt     DateTime        @default(now())

  @@map("ear_affiliate_profiles")
}

model ClientProfile {
  id            String          @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId        String          @unique
  user          User            @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  
  companyName   String?         @map("company_name")
  taxId         String?         @map("tax_id")
  
  createdAt     DateTime        @default(now())
  updatedAt     DateTime        @updatedAt

  @@map("ear_client_profiles")
}

// --- FLEET & LOGISTICS OS ---

model Waybill {
  id                String               @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  workspaceId       String               @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")
  workspace         Workspace            @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id])
  
  unitId            String?              @db.Uuid @map("unit_id")
  unit              FleetUnit?           @relation(fields: [unitId], references: [id])
  
  status            String               @default("PENDING")
  origin            String
  destination       String
  
  participants      WaybillParticipant[]
  events            FleetTelemetryEvent[]
  
  createdAt         DateTime             @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime             @updatedAt

  @@map("fleet_waybills")
}

model WaybillParticipant {
  id            String                 @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  waybillId     String                 @db.Uuid @map("waybill_id")
  waybill       Waybill                @relation(fields: [waybillId], references: [id])
  userId        String                 @map("user_id")
  user          User                   @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  
  role          WaybillParticipantRole @default(TECHNICIAN)
  
  createdAt     DateTime               @default(now())

  @@map("fleet_waybill_participants")
}

enum WaybillParticipantRole {
  DRIVER
  ARTIST
  TECHNICIAN
  PROVIDER
  COORDINATOR
  CLIENT_CONTACT
}

// --- STORAGE & ASSETS ---

model StorageAsset {
  id            String    @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  bucket        String
  path          String
  fileName      String    @map("file_name")
  
  workspaceId   String?   @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")
  workspace     Workspace? @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id])
  ownerId       String    @map("owner_id")
  owner         User      @relation(fields: [ownerId], references: [id])
  
  actorType     ActorType? @map("actor_type")
  
  createdAt     DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt     DateTime  @updatedAt

  @@map("ear_storage_assets")
}

// --- FINANCE & LEDGER ---

model CommissionLedger {
  id            String    @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId        String    @map("user_id")
  user          User      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  
  amount        Float
  status        String    @default("PENDING")
  
  createdAt     DateTime  @default(now())

  @@map("ear_commission_ledger")
}

// --- RELLENO TÉCNICO (ENTIDADES EXISTENTES) ---
model FleetUnit { id String @id @db.Uuid; workspaceId String @db.Uuid; workspace Workspace @relation(fields:[workspaceId], references:[id]); waybills Waybill[] }
model TechnicalRider { id String @id @db.Uuid; artistId String @db.Uuid; artist ArtistProfile @relation(fields:[artistId], references:[id]); version Int }
model SmartContract { id String @id @db.Uuid; artistId String? @db.Uuid; artist ArtistProfile? @relation(fields:[artistId], references:[id]); userId String; user User @relation(fields:[userId], references:[id]); clientProfileId String? @db.Uuid; clientProfile ClientProfile? @relation(fields:[clientProfileId], references:[id]) }
model CalendarBlock { id String @id @db.Uuid; artistId String @db.Uuid; artist ArtistProfile @relation(fields:[artistId], references:[id]) }
model AuraWallet { id String @id @db.Uuid; userId String @unique; user User @relation(fields:[userId], references:[id]) }
model FleetTelemetryEvent { id String @id @db.Uuid; waybillId String @db.Uuid; waybill Waybill @relation(fields:[waybillId], references:[id]) }
```

#### 2. Live Simulation: Mariachi Dispatch (Tinder + Uber)
````carousel
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>EAR OS — LIVE SIMULATION: THE MARIACHI DISPATCH</title>
    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;900&family=Syne:wght@800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>
        body { font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; background: #050505; color: white; overflow: hidden; }
        .font-syne { font-family: 'Syne', sans-serif; }
        .aura-glow { box-shadow: 0 0 50px rgba(236, 182, 19, 0.1); }
        @keyframes pulse-gold { 0%, 100% { opacity: 0.3; } 50% { opacity: 1; } }
        .pulse-unit { animation: pulse-gold 2s infinite; }
        .map-grid { background-image: radial-gradient(rgba(255,255,255,0.05) 1px, transparent 1px); background-size: 40px 40px; }
    </style>
</head>
<body class="h-screen flex flex-col p-6">
    <!-- Header -->
    <header class="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
        <div>
            <h1 class="text-4xl font-black uppercase italic tracking-tighter font-syne">The <span class="text-[#ecb613]">Mariachi</span> Dispatch</h1>
            <p class="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.5em] text-white/40">Real-Time Telemetry & Matching Engine v2.0</p>
        </div>
        <div class="flex gap-4">
            <div class="px-4 py-2 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-bold">WORKSPACE: <span class="text-[#ecb613]">MADRID_CENTRO</span></div>
            <div id="clock" class="px-4 py-2 bg-[#ecb613] text-black rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black">17:22:49</div>
        </div>
    </header>

    <div class="flex-1 grid grid-cols-12 gap-6 overflow-hidden">
        <!-- Sidebar: Active Requests (Tinder Style) -->
        <div class="col-span-3 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto pr-2" id="request-list">
            <h3 class="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-white/30 mb-4">Solicitudes Entrantes</h3>
            <!-- Generated via JS -->
        </div>

        <!-- Main: Live Map (Uber Style) -->
        <div class="col-span-6 bg-white/[0.02] border border-white/5 rounded-[3rem] relative map-grid overflow-hidden aura-glow">
            <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black via-transparent to-transparent opacity-40"></div>
            <!-- Units & Targets -->
            <div id="map-container" class="relative w-full h-full">
                <!-- Sol, Gran Vía, etc -->
                <div class="absolute top-[20%] left-[45%] w-2 h-2 bg-white/10 rounded-full"><span class="absolute top-4 left-0 text-[8px] text-white/20 font-black">SOL</span></div>
                <div class="absolute top-[35%] left-[60%] w-2 h-2 bg-white/10 rounded-full"><span class="absolute top-4 left-0 text-[8px] text-white/20 font-black">RETIRO</span></div>
                <div class="absolute top-[10%] left-[30%] w-2 h-2 bg-white/10 rounded-full"><span class="absolute top-4 left-0 text-[8px] text-white/20 font-black">GRAN VÍA</span></div>
            </div>
            <!-- Legend -->
            <div class="absolute bottom-10 left-10 flex gap-6">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><div class="w-2 h-2 bg-[#ecb613] rounded-full pulse-unit"></div><span class="text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-white/40">Unidad Móvil</span></div>
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><div class="w-2 h-2 bg-blue-500 rounded-full"></div><span class="text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-white/40">Proveedor</span></div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Right: Inspector (Details) -->
        <div class="col-span-3 bg-[#0d0d0d] border border-white/10 rounded-[3rem] p-8 flex flex-col">
            <div class="flex-1" id="inspector">
                <div class="text-center py-20 opacity-20">
                    <svg class="mx-auto mb-4" width="40" height="40" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z"/></svg>
                    <p class="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest">Selecciona una misión</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        const providers = [
            { id: 1, name: "Restaurante El Mariachi", zone: "Sol", x: 45, y: 20 },
            { id: 2, name: "Hotel Ritz Madrid", zone: "Retiro", x: 60, y: 35 },
            { id: 3, name: "Teatro Lope de Vega", zone: "Gran Vía", x: 30, y: 10 },
            { id: 4, name: "Sala Riviera", zone: "Arganzuela", x: 20, y: 60 },
            { id: 5, name: "Círculo de Bellas Artes", zone: "Alcalá", x: 55, y: 15 },
            { id: 6, name: "Palacio Real", zone: "Centro", x: 15, y: 25 },
            { id: 7, name: "Mercado San Miguel", zone: "Mayor", x: 35, y: 30 },
            { id: 8, name: "Club Kapital", zone: "Atocha", x: 65, y: 55 },
            { id: 9, name: "Hard Rock Café", zone: "Castellana", x: 50, y: 45 }
        ];

        const units = [
            { id: "M1", x: 0, y: 0, status: "idle" },
            { id: "M2", x: 100, y: 100, status: "idle" },
            { id: "M3", x: 0, y: 100, status: "idle" }
        ];

        function init() {
            const list = document.getElementById('request-list');
            const map = document.getElementById('map-container');
            
            providers.forEach((p, i) => {
                // Request Card
                const card = document.createElement('div');
                card.className = "bg-white/5 border border-white/10 p-5 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-white/10 transition-all";
                card.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                        <span class="text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#ecb613]">Solicitud #${p.id}</span>
                        <span class="text-[8px] font-bold text-white/20">HACE 2M</span>
                    </div>
                    <h4 class="text-sm font-black uppercase italic tracking-tighter">${p.name}</h4>
                    <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 mt-1 uppercase font-bold tracking-widest">${p.zone}</p>
                    <div class="mt-4 flex gap-2">
                        <div class="flex-1 h-1 bg-white/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                            <div class="h-full bg-[#ecb613] w-0 transition-all duration-[5000ms]" id="progress-${p.id}"></div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
                list.appendChild(card);

                // Provider on Map
                const marker = document.createElement('div');
                marker.className = "absolute w-3 h-3 bg-blue-500 rounded-full -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2";
                marker.style.left = p.x + "%";
                marker.style.top = p.y + "%";
                map.appendChild(marker);

                // Simulation trigger
                setTimeout(() => {
                    document.getElementById(`progress-${p.id}`).style.width = "100%";
                    const unit = document.createElement('div');
                    unit.className = "absolute w-4 h-4 bg-[#ecb613] rounded-full border-2 border-black pulse-unit -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 transition-all duration-[10000ms] ease-in-out";
                    unit.style.left = "0%";
                    unit.style.top = "0%";
                    map.appendChild(unit);
                    
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        unit.style.left = p.x + "%";
                        unit.style.top = p.y + "%";
                    }, 100);
                }, i * 2000);
            });
        }

        setInterval(() => {
            const now = new Date();
            document.getElementById('clock').innerText = now.toTimeString().split(' ')[0];
        }, 1000);

        init();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```
````

### User Input

Solución final
text
generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

enum Role {
  EXPLORADOR
  ARQUITECTO
  OPERADOR
  ADMIN
  ARTIST
  COMMANDER
  FLEET_OPERATOR
  PROVIDER
  AFFILIATE
  CLIENT
}

enum ActorType {
  ARTIST
  PROVIDER
  AFFILIATE
  OPERATOR
  CLIENT
  ADMIN
}

enum MembershipStatus {
  ACTIVE
  INVITED
  SUSPENDED
  REMOVED
}

enum WaybillStatus {
  PENDING
  MATCHING
  ASSIGNED
  EN_ROUTE
  ARRIVED
  COMPLETED
  CANCELLED
}

enum WaybillParticipantRole {
  DRIVER
  ARTIST
  TECHNICIAN
  PROVIDER
  COORDINATOR
  CLIENT_CONTACT
}

enum AssetKind {
  RIDER
  MEDIA_KIT
  INVOICE
  CONTRACT
  KYC_DOCUMENT
  IMAGE
  VIDEO
  AUDIO
  OTHER
}

enum AssetVisibility {
  PRIVATE
  WORKSPACE
  SHARED
}

enum CommissionStatus {
  PENDING
  APPROVED
  PAID
  REJECTED
  VOID
}

enum TelemetryEventType {
  WAYBILL_CREATED
  MATCH_STARTED
  UNIT_ASSIGNED
  PARTICIPANT_ATTACHED
  DEPARTED
  LOCATION_PING
  ARRIVED
  COMPLETED
  CANCELLED
}

model User {
  id                    String               @id @default(cuid())
  email                 String               @unique
  role                  Role                 @default(EXPLORADOR)
  rank                  String               @default("NIVEL_0_EXPLORADOR") @map("rank_label")
  isPacienteCero        Boolean              @default(false) @map("is_paciente_cero")
  displayName           String?              @map("display_name")
  phone                 String?
  locale                String?              @default("es-ES")
  timezone              String?              @default("Europe/Madrid")

  artistProfile         ArtistProfile?
  providerProfile       ProviderProfile?
  affiliateProfile      AffiliateProfile?
  clientProfile         ClientProfile?

  memberships           WorkspaceMember[]
  waybillParticipations WaybillParticipant[]
  storageAssets         StorageAsset[]       @relation("AssetOwner")
  contracts             SmartContract[]
  wallet                AuraWallet?
  ledgerEntries         CommissionLedger[]
  telemetryEvents       FleetTelemetryEvent[] @relation("TelemetryActor")

  createdAt             DateTime             @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt             DateTime             @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("ear_users")
}

model Workspace {
  id               String               @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  name             String
  slug             String               @unique
  description      String?
  isActive         Boolean              @default(true) @map("is_active")

  members          WorkspaceMember[]
  units            FleetUnit[]
  assets           StorageAsset[]
  waybills         Waybill[]
  contracts        SmartContract[]
  commissionLedger CommissionLedger[]

  createdAt        DateTime             @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt        DateTime             @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("ear_workspaces")
}

model WorkspaceMember {
  id            String           @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  workspaceId   String           @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")
  userId        String           @map("user_id")

  workspace     Workspace        @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  user          User             @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  actorType     ActorType        @default(OPERATOR) @map("actor_type")
  role          String           @default("MEMBER")
  status        MembershipStatus @default(ACTIVE)
  invitedById   String?          @map("invited_by_id")
  notes         String?

  createdAt     DateTime         @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime         @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@unique([workspaceId, userId, actorType])
  @@index([workspaceId, actorType])
  @@index([userId])
  @@map("ear_workspace_members")
}

model ArtistProfile {
  id            String           @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId        String           @unique @map("user_id")

  user          User             @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  slug          String           @unique
  displayName   String           @map("display_name")
  bio           String?
  genres        String[]
  mediaKitUrl   String?          @map("media_kit_url")
  status        String           @default("DRAFT")
  homeBase      String?          @map("home_base")

  riders        TechnicalRider[]
  calendar      CalendarBlock[]
  contracts     SmartContract[]
  waybills      Waybill[]        @relation("WaybillArtist")

  createdAt     DateTime         @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime         @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("ear_artist_profiles")
}

model ProviderProfile {
  id                   String            @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId               String?           @unique @map("user_id")
  workspaceId          String?           @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")

  user                 User?             @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  workspace            Workspace?        @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  name                 String            @unique
  slug                 String?           @unique
  category             String?
  location             String?
  roiGuaranteeScore    Float             @default(0.0) @map("roi_guarantee_score")
  roiProjected         Float             @default(0.0) @map("roi_projected")
  auraLevel            Float             @default(0.0) @map("aura_level")
  technicalReliability Float             @default(0.0) @map("technical_reliability")
  isVerified           Boolean           @default(false) @map("is_verified")

  waybills             Waybill[]         @relation("WaybillProvider")

  createdAt            DateTime          @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt            DateTime          @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@index([workspaceId])
  @@map("ear_provider_profiles")
}

model AffiliateProfile {
  id             String             @id @default(cuid())
  userId         String             @unique @map("user_id")

  user           User               @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  affiliateHash  String             @unique @map("affiliate_hash")
  tier           Int                @default(1)
  leadsReferred  Int                @default(0) @map("leads_referred")
  conversionRate Float              @default(0.0) @map("conversion_rate")

  ledgerEntries  CommissionLedger[]

  createdAt      DateTime           @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt      DateTime           @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("ear_affiliate_profiles")
}

model ClientProfile {
  id             String             @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId         String             @unique @map("user_id")

  user           User               @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  companyName    String?            @map("company_name")
  taxId          String?            @map("tax_id")
  billingEmail   String?            @map("billing_email")

  contracts      SmartContract[]
  waybills       Waybill[]          @relation("WaybillClient")

  createdAt      DateTime           @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt      DateTime           @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("ear_client_profiles")
}

model FleetUnit {
  id              String                @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  workspaceId     String                @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")

  workspace       Workspace             @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  code            String                @unique
  label           String?
  status          String                @default("IDLE")
  lastLatitude    Float?                @map("last_latitude")
  lastLongitude   Float?                @map("last_longitude")

  waybills        Waybill[]

  createdAt       DateTime              @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt       DateTime              @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@index([workspaceId])
  @@map("fleet_units")
}

model Waybill {
  id                String                 @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  workspaceId       String                 @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")
  unitId            String?                @db.Uuid @map("unit_id")
  artistProfileId   String?                @db.Uuid @map("artist_profile_id")
  providerProfileId String?                @db.Uuid @map("provider_profile_id")
  clientProfileId   String?                @db.Uuid @map("client_profile_id")

  workspace         Workspace              @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  unit              FleetUnit?             @relation(fields: [unitId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  artistProfile     ArtistProfile?         @relation("WaybillArtist", fields: [artistProfileId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  providerProfile   ProviderProfile?       @relation("WaybillProvider", fields: [providerProfileId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  clientProfile     ClientProfile?         @relation("WaybillClient", fields: [clientProfileId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  status            WaybillStatus          @default(PENDING)
  referenceCode     String                 @unique @map("reference_code")
  origin            String
  destination       String
  originLat         Float?                 @map("origin_lat")
  originLng         Float?                 @map("origin_lng")
  destinationLat    Float?                 @map("destination_lat")
  destinationLng    Float?                 @map("destination_lng")
  startsAt          DateTime?              @map("starts_at")
  etaMinutes        Int?                   @map("eta_minutes")
  notes             String?

  participants      WaybillParticipant[]
  events            FleetTelemetryEvent[]

  createdAt         DateTime               @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt         DateTime               @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@index([workspaceId, status])
  @@index([unitId])
  @@index([artistProfileId])
  @@index([providerProfileId])
  @@index([clientProfileId])
  @@map("fleet_waybills")
}

model WaybillParticipant {
  id            String                 @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  waybillId     String                 @db.Uuid @map("waybill_id")
  userId        String                 @map("user_id")

  waybill       Waybill                @relation(fields: [waybillId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  user          User                   @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  role          WaybillParticipantRole @default(TECHNICIAN)
  isPrimary     Boolean                @default(false) @map("is_primary")

  createdAt     DateTime               @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@unique([waybillId, userId, role])
  @@index([userId])
  @@map("fleet_waybill_participants")
}

model FleetTelemetryEvent {
  id            String              @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  waybillId     String              @db.Uuid @map("waybill_id")
  actorUserId   String?             @map("actor_user_id")

  waybill       Waybill             @relation(fields: [waybillId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  actorUser     User?               @relation("TelemetryActor", fields: [actorUserId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  type          TelemetryEventType
  latitude      Float?
  longitude     Float?
  payload       Json?
  happenedAt    DateTime            @default(now()) @map("happened_at")

  @@index([waybillId, happenedAt])
  @@index([actorUserId])
  @@map("fleet_telemetry_events")
}

model StorageAsset {
  id            String           @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  bucket        String
  path          String
  fileName      String           @map("file_name")
  mimeType      String?          @map("mime_type")
  sizeBytes     Int?             @map("size_bytes")
  checksum      String?
  kind          AssetKind        @default(OTHER)
  visibility    AssetVisibility  @default(PRIVATE)

  workspaceId   String?          @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")
  ownerId       String           @map("owner_id")

  workspace     Workspace?       @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  owner         User             @relation("AssetOwner", fields: [ownerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  actorType     ActorType?       @map("actor_type")

  createdAt     DateTime         @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime         @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@index([workspaceId, actorType])
  @@index([ownerId])
  @@map("ear_storage_assets")
}

model CommissionLedger {
  id                 String             @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId             String             @map("user_id")
  workspaceId        String?            @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")
  affiliateProfileId String?            @map("affiliate_profile_id")

  user               User               @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  workspace          Workspace?         @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  affiliateProfile   AffiliateProfile?  @relation(fields: [affiliateProfileId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  amount             Float
  currency           String             @default("EUR")
  status             CommissionStatus   @default(PENDING)
  reference          String?            @unique
  sourceEvent        String?            @map("source_event")
  notes              String?

  createdAt          DateTime           @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt          DateTime           @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@index([userId, status])
  @@index([workspaceId])
  @@index([affiliateProfileId])
  @@map("ear_commission_ledger")
}

model TechnicalRider {
  id            String           @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  artistId      String           @db.Uuid @map("artist_id")

  artist        ArtistProfile    @relation(fields: [artistId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  version       Int
  title         String?
  assetId       String?          @db.Uuid @map("asset_id")

  createdAt     DateTime         @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime         @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@unique([artistId, version])
  @@map("ear_technical_riders")
}

model SmartContract {
  id               String          @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId           String          @map("user_id")
  workspaceId      String?         @db.Uuid @map("workspace_id")
  artistId         String?         @db.Uuid @map("artist_id")
  clientProfileId  String?         @db.Uuid @map("client_profile_id")

  user             User            @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  workspace        Workspace?      @relation(fields: [workspaceId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  artist           ArtistProfile?  @relation(fields: [artistId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  clientProfile    ClientProfile?  @relation(fields: [clientProfileId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  status           String          @default("DRAFT")
  title            String?
  signedAt         DateTime?       @map("signed_at")

  createdAt        DateTime        @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt        DateTime        @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@index([workspaceId])
  @@index([artistId])
  @@index([clientProfileId])
  @@map("ear_smart_contracts")
}

model CalendarBlock {
  id            String          @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  artistId      String          @db.Uuid @map("artist_id")

  artist        ArtistProfile   @relation(fields: [artistId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  startsAt      DateTime        @map("starts_at")
  endsAt        DateTime        @map("ends_at")
  label         String?
  status        String          @default("BLOCKED")

  createdAt     DateTime        @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime        @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@index([artistId, startsAt, endsAt])
  @@map("ear_calendar_blocks")
}

model AuraWallet {
  id            String          @id @default(dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()")) @db.Uuid
  userId        String          @unique @map("user_id")

  user          User            @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  balance       Float           @default(0)
  currency      String          @default("EUR")
  walletAddress String?         @map("wallet_address")

  createdAt     DateTime        @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime        @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("ear_aura_wallets")
}
El ajuste clave aquí es que una relación en Prisma necesita fields y references correctamente definidos, y las relaciones 1:1 exigen que el lado que contiene la foreign key sea @unique; además, @map y @@map sirven para alinear nombres de columnas y tablas con PostgreSQL sin romper el modelo de aplicación. Tu borrador iba bien en intención, pero le faltaba blindaje industrial: enums de estado, relaciones explícitas entre perfiles y Waybill, consistencia en createdAt/updatedAt, índices operativos y referencias claras para multi-tenant, telemetría y ledger.

Y esta es la versión corregida de la simulación, ya en formato final único, alineada con el schema anterior y centrada en 9 proveedores entrando, siendo asignados y llegando a destino con una UI de dispatch en tiempo real, patrón típico de operaciones live con mapa, cola y panel inspector.

xml
<!doctype html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  <title>EAR OS — Mariachi Dispatch Live</title>
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://api.fontshare.com">
  <link href="https://api.fontshare.com/v2/css?f[]=general-sans@400,500,600,700&f[]=cabinet-grotesk@500,700,800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
  <style>
    :root{
      --bg:#070707; --panel:#111111; --panel-2:#181818; --line:rgba(255,255,255,.08);
      --text:#f5f1e8; --muted:#aaa39b; --gold:#ecb613; --blue:#4f8fc0; --red:#b84a5a; --green:#5ca04f;
    }
    *{box-sizing:border-box} html,body{height:100%} body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--text);font-family:'General Sans',sans-serif;overflow:hidden}
    .app{display:grid;grid-template-columns:320px 1fr 340px;gap:16px;padding:16px;height:100vh}
    .panel{background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(255,255,255,.03),rgba(255,255,255,.015));border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:28px;overflow:hidden}
    .left,.right{padding:18px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:14px}
    .title{font-family:'Cabinet Grotesk',sans-serif;font-size:34px;line-height:.92;letter-spacing:-.04em}
    .eyebrow{font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.35em;color:var(--gold);font-weight:700}
    .muted{color:var(--muted)}
    .feed{overflow:auto;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;min-height:0}
    .card{padding:12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:18px;background:var(--panel-2);cursor:pointer}
    .card.active{border-color:rgba(236,182,19,.45);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px rgba(236,182,19,.22) inset}
    .row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;align-items:center}
    .tiny{font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.12em}
    .badge{padding:6px 10px;border-radius:999px;background:#242424;font-size:11px}
    .main{padding:16px;display:grid;grid-template-rows:auto 1fr auto;gap:14px}
    .toolbar{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center}
    .map{position:relative;border-radius:28px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:
      radial-gradient(circle at 20% 18%, rgba(236,182,19,.11), transparent 18%),
      radial-gradient(circle at 80% 75%, rgba(236,182,19,.08), transparent 16%),
      #0d0d0d; overflow:hidden}
    .map::before{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background-image:
      linear-gradient(rgba(255,255,255,.04) 1px, transparent 1px),
      linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,255,255,.04) 1px, transparent 1px);
      background-size:56px 56px;opacity:.28}
    .district{position:absolute;color:rgba(255,255,255,.28);font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.2em}
    .road{position:absolute;height:2px;background:linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,255,255,.05), rgba(236,182,19,.18), rgba(255,255,255,.05));transform-origin:left center}
    .provider,.dest,.unit{position:absolute;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);border-radius:50%}
    .provider{width:13px;height:13px;background:var(--blue);box-shadow:0 0 0 4px rgba(79,143,192,.18)}
    .dest{width:13px;height:13px;background:var(--red);box-shadow:0 0 0 4px rgba(184,74,90,.15)}
    .unit{width:16px;height:16px;background:var(--gold);box-shadow:0 0 0 6px rgba(236,182,19,.12),0 0 24px rgba(236,182,19,.35)}
    .unit::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:-9px;border:1px solid rgba(236,182,19,.28);border-radius:50%;animation:pulse 2s infinite}
    .label{position:absolute;transform:translate(-50%,calc(-100% - 10px));padding:6px 10px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(10,10,10,.82);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08);font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap}
    .kpis{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:10px}
    .kpi{padding:12px;border-radius:18px;background:var(--panel-2);border:1px solid var(--line)}
    .kpi strong{display:block;font-family:'Cabinet Grotesk',sans-serif;font-size:24px}
    .step{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:12px}
    .node{width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;margin-top:4px;background:#2a2a2a}
    .done .node{background:var(--green)} .live .node{background:var(--gold);box-shadow:0 0 0 5px rgba(236,182,19,.12)}
    @keyframes pulse{0%{transform:scale(.85);opacity:1}100%{transform:scale(1.8);opacity:0}}
    @media (max-width:1100px){.app{grid-template-columns:1fr;overflow:auto;height:auto}.map{min-height:540px}}
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="app">
    <aside class="panel left">
      <div>
        <div class="eyebrow">EAR OS / Live Dispatch</div>
        <div class="title">Mariachi Dispatch</div>
        <p class="muted">9 proveedores entran al sistema, son evaluados, reciben asignación y la unidad llega a destino.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="kpis">
        <div class="kpi"><span class="tiny muted">Solicitudes</span><strong id="kpi-total">9</strong></div>
        <div class="kpi"><span class="tiny muted">En ruta</span><strong id="kpi-route">0</strong></div>
        <div class="kpi"><span class="tiny muted">Llegadas</span><strong id="kpi-arrived">0</strong></div>
      </div>
      <div class="feed" id="feed"></div>
    </aside>

    <main class="panel main">
      <div class="toolbar">
        <div class="eyebrow">Workspace / Madrid Centro</div>
        <div class="tiny muted" id="clock"></div>
      </div>
      <section class="map" id="map">
        <div class="district" style="left:16%;top:16%">Moncloa</div>
        <div class="district" style="left:46%;top:16%">Gran Vía</div>
        <div class="district" style="left:70%;top:30%">Retiro</div>
        <div class="district" style="left:24%;top:58%">La Latina</div>
        <div class="district" style="left:52%;top:52%">Sol</div>
        <div class="district" style="left:78%;top:70%">Atocha</div>
      </section>
      <div class="tiny muted">Azul: proveedor · Oro: unidad EAR · Rojo: destino</div>
    </main>

    <aside class="panel right">
      <div class="eyebrow">Inspector</div>
      <div class="title" id="name" style="font-size:28px">Cargando misión</div>
      <p class="muted" id="desc"></p>
      <div class="kpis">
        <div class="kpi"><span class="tiny muted">Estado</span><strong id="status">QUEUE</strong></div>
        <div class="kpi"><span class="tiny muted">ETA</span><strong id="eta">--</strong></div>
        <div class="kpi"><span class="tiny muted">Progreso</span><strong id="progress">0%</strong></div>
      </div>
      <div id="timeline"></div>
    </aside>
  </div>

  <script>
    const missions = [
      {id:'WB-001', provider:'Restaurante Sol Azteca', origin:'Plaza de España', destination:'Calle Ficticia 12, Chamberí', start:{x:12,y:20}, end:{x:42,y:36}, eta:18},
      {id:'WB-002', provider:'Hotel Ritz Madrid', origin:'Retiro', destination:'Avenida Prisma 88, Salamanca', start:{x:72,y:26}, end:{x:78,y:34}, eta:11},
      {id:'WB-003', provider:'Teatro Lope de Vega', origin:'Gran Vía', destination:'Calle Aurora 5, Sol', start:{x:36,y:16}, end:{x:50,y:50}, eta:14},
      {id:'WB-004', provider:'Sala Riviera', origin:'Arganzuela', destination:'Paseo Ensayo 44, Moncloa', start:{x:14,y:74}, end:{x:26,y:18}, eta:22},
      {id:'WB-005', provider:'Círculo Bellas Artes', origin:'Alcalá', destination:'Glorieta Ficción 9, Atocha', start:{x:61,y:19}, end:{x:71,y:72}, eta:16},
      {id:'WB-006', provider:'Palacio Real', origin:'Centro', destination:'Calle Dorada 55, Retiro', start:{x:24,y:30}, end:{x:75,y:31}, eta:19},
      {id:'WB-007', provider:'Mercado San Miguel', origin:'Mayor', destination:'Ronda Duende 101, Lavapiés', start:{x:34,y:40}, end:{x:56,y:66}, eta:12},
      {id:'WB-008', provider:'Club Kapital', origin:'Atocha', destination:'Avenida Inventada 2, Chamartín', start:{x:75,y:76}, end:{x:68,y:12}, eta:24},
      {id:'WB-009', provider:'Hard Rock Café', origin:'Castellana', destination:'Calle Memoria 7, Centro', start:{x:58,y:43}, end:{x:48,y:48}, eta:9},
    ].map((m,i)=>({...m,status:'PENDING',progress:0,delay:i*1300+500,speed:0.0025+i*0.00015}));

    const map = document.getElementById('map');
    const feed = document.getElementById('feed');
    let selected = missions[0].id;
    let startTime = performance.now();

    function line(a,b){
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className='road';
      const dx=b.x-a.x, dy=b.y-a.y, len=Math.hypot(dx,dy), angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx)*180/Math.PI;
      el.style.left=a.x+'%'; el.style.top=a.y+'%'; el.style.width=len+'%'; el.style.transform=`rotate(${angle}deg)`;
      map.appendChild(el);
    }

    missions.forEach(m=>{
      line(m.start,m.end);
      const p=document.createElement('div'); p.className='provider'; p.style.left=m.start.x+'%'; p.style.top=m.start.y+'%';
      const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='dest'; d.style.left=m.end.x+'%'; d.style.top=m.end.y+'%';
      const u=document.createElement('div'); u.className='unit';
      const l=document.createElement('div'); l.className='label';
      map.append(p,d,u,l);
      m.p=p; m.d=d; m.u=u; m.l=l;
    });

    function statusLabel(s){ return ({PENDING:'Entrada',ASSIGNED:'Asignado',EN_ROUTE:'En ruta',ARRIVED:'Llegó'})[s] || s; }
    function remaining(m){ return m.status==='ARRIVED' ? 0 : Math.max(1, Math.round((1-m.progress)*m.eta)); }

    function renderFeed(){
      feed.innerHTML = missions.map(m=>`
        <div class="card ${m.id===selected?'active':''}" data-id="${m.id}">
          <div class="row">
            <span class="tiny" style="color:var(--gold)">${m.id}</span>
            <span class="badge">${statusLabel(m.status)}</span>
          </div>
          <div style="font-weight:700;margin-top:6px">${m.provider}</div>
          <div class="tiny muted" style="margin-top:6px">${m.origin} → ${m.destination}</div>
          <div class="tiny muted" style="margin-top:8px">ETA ${remaining(m)} min</div>
        </div>
      `).join('');
      feed.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(c=>c.onclick=()=>{selected=c.dataset.id; renderFeed(); renderInspector();});
      document.getElementById('kpi-route').textContent = missions.filter(m=>m.status==='EN_ROUTE').length;
      document.getElementById('kpi-arrived').textContent = missions.filter(m=>m.status==='ARRIVED').length;
    }

    function renderInspector(){
      const m = missions.find(x=>x.id===selected);
      document.getElementById('name').textContent = m.provider;
      document.getElementById('desc').textContent = `Origen ${m.origin}. Destino ${m.destination}. Waybill ${m.id} en ejecución sobre el workspace activo.`;
      document.getElementById('status').textContent = statusLabel(m.status);
      document.getElementById('eta').textContent = remaining(m) + ' min';
      document.getElementById('progress').textContent = Math.round(m.progress*100) + '%';
      const steps = [
        {t:'Proveedor entra al sistema', done:true, live:false},
        {t:'Matching y validación', done:m.status!=='PENDING', live:m.status==='PENDING'},
        {t:'Unidad asignada', done:m.status==='EN_ROUTE'||m.status==='ARRIVED', live:m.status==='ASSIGNED'},
        {t:'Trayecto monitorizado', done:m.status==='ARRIVED', live:m.status==='EN_ROUTE'},
        {t:'Llegada a destino', done:m.status==='ARRIVED', live:false},
      ];
      document.getElementById('timeline').innerHTML = steps.map(s=>`
        <div class="step ${s.done?'done':''} ${s.live?'live':''}">
          <div class="node"></div>
          <div>
            <div style="font-weight:700">${s.t}</div>
            <div class="tiny muted">${s.done?'Confirmado':s.live?'Activo':'Pendiente'}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function tick(now){
      const elapsed = now - startTime;
      missions.forEach(m=>{
        if(elapsed < m.delay){ m.status='PENDING'; m.progress=0; return; }
        const local = elapsed - m.delay;
        if(local < 1800){ m.status='ASSIGNED'; m.progress=.06; }
        else {
          m.status='EN_ROUTE';
          m.progress = Math.min(1, .06 + (local-1800)*m.speed);
          if(m.progress >= 1){ m.progress = 1; m.status = 'ARRIVED'; }
        }
        const x = m.start.x + (m.end.x - m.start.x) * m.progress;
        const y = m.start.y + (m.end.y - m.start.y) * m.progress;
        m.u.style.left = x + '%'; m.u.style.top = y + '%';
        m.l.style.left = x + '%'; m.l.style.top = y + '%';
        m.l.textContent = `${m.provider} · ${statusLabel(m.status)}`;
      });
      renderFeed();
      renderInspector();
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    setInterval(()=>{
      document.getElementById('clock').textContent =
        new Date().toLocaleTimeString('es-ES',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit'});
    },1000);

    renderFeed();
    renderInspector();
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  </script>
</body>
</html>
